## Overview

This catalog is a generated inventory of the repository skills currently published in the AREX-Skill Library. It is organized by the canonical `area -> family` taxonomy. A repository appears once for every exact family assignment, so repositories with multiple evidence-backed capabilities intentionally occur in multiple tables.

The machine-readable sources of truth are [`repository-index.jsonl`](../skills/repositories/repo-skills/repository-index.jsonl), [`repositories.jsonl`](../skills/repositories/repo-skills-router/references/index/repositories.jsonl), and [`assignments.jsonl`](../skills/repositories/repo-skills-router/references/index/assignments.jsonl). The repository skill root links below open the progressive-disclosure entry point; individual sub-skills are intentionally not expanded here.

The commit shown beside each GitHub link is the source snapshot used to build the skill. It is displayed using only the first seven characters; when a full SHA is recorded, the link targets that full commit, and when only an abbreviated source identifier is available, the link uses its displayed prefix. `commit not recorded` means that neither the repository index nor the skill provenance contains a usable source snapshot identifier.

Current scope: **1,000 repository skill roots**, **20 areas**, **178 families**, and **2,204 area-family memberships**.
